"\*\*\*" indicates [King of Númenor](/wiki/King_of_N%C3%BAmenor) or [High King of the Dúnedain](/wiki/High_King_of_the_D%C3%BAnedain)<br> "\*\*" indicates [King of Arnor](/wiki/King_of_Arnor)<br> "\*" indicates [King of Arthedain](/wiki/King_of_Arthedain)<br> "#" indicates [King of Gondor](/wiki/King_of_Gondor)<br>
